</div>\\n</div>\\n\\n<script>\\n(function(){\\n  const shopname="test
shop";\\n  const defaultinvitee="test agent";\\n  const
form=document.getelementbyid(\\'inviteform\\');\\n  const
nameinput=document.getelementbyid(\\'name\\');\\n  const
greeting=document.getelementbyid(\\'greeting\\');\\n  const
p1=document.getelementbyid(\\'id_password1\\');\\n  const
p2=document.getelementbyid(\\'id_password2\\');\\n  const
matchhint=document.getelementbyid(\\'matchhint\\');\\n  const
strengthhint=document.getelementbyid(\\'strengthhint\\');\\n  const
expirybadge=document.getelementbyid(\\'expirybadge\\');\\n  const
submitbtn=document.getelementbyid(\\'submitbtn\\');\\n\\n  // update greeting
when name changes\\n  function updategreeting(){\\n
if(!greeting||!nameinput) return;\\n    const
name=nameinput.value.trim()||defaultinvitee||\\'there\\';\\n
greeting.textcontent=`hi ${name}, welcome to ${shopname}.`;\\n  }\\n\\n  //
password strength\\n  function strength(s){\\n    let n=0;\\n
if(s.length>=12)n++;\\n    if(/[a-z]/.test(s)&&/[a-z]/.test(s))n++;\\n
if(/\\\\d/.test(s))n++;\\n    if(/[^a-za-z0-9]/.test(s))n++;\\n    return
n;\\n  }\\n  function updatestrength(){\\n    if(!p1||!strengthhint)return;\\n
   const sc=strength(p1.value||"");\\n    let msg="";\\n    if(!p1.value)
msg="";\\n    else if(sc<=1) msg="\u26a0\ufe0f too weak - needs 12+ chars,
upper, lower, digit, symbol";\\n    else if(sc===2) msg="\u26a0\ufe0f weak -
missing some requirements";\\n    else if(sc===3) msg="\u2713 good
password";\\n    else msg="\u2713 strong password";\\n
strengthhint.textcontent=msg;\\n    strengthhint.classname=(sc<=2?"text-danger"
:sc===3?"text-warning":"text-success");\\n  }\\n\\n  // password match\\n
function updatematch(){\\n    if(!p1||!p2||!matchhint)return;\\n
if(!p1.value||!p2.value){matchhint.style.display=\\'none\\';return;}\\n
const ok=p1.value===p2.value && p1.value.length>=12;\\n
matchhint.style.display=\\'block\\';\\n    matchhint.classname="form-text
"+(ok?"text-success":"text-danger");\\n    matchhint.textcontent=ok?"\u2713
passwords match":"\u26a0\ufe0f passwords don\\'t match";\\n  }\\n\\n  //
show/hide password toggles\\n  document.queryselectorall(\\'button[data-toggle=
"reveal"]\\').foreach(btn=>{\\n    btn.addeventlistener(\\'click\\',()=>{\\n
   const sel=btn.getattribute(\\'data-target\\');\\n      const
input=document.queryselector(sel);\\n      if(!input)return;\\n      const
ispwd=input.getattribute(\\'type\\')===\\'password\\';\\n
input.setattribute(\\'type\\',ispwd?\\'text\\':\\'password\\');\\n
btn.textcontent=ispwd?\\'hide\\':\\'show\\';\\n    });\\n  });\\n\\n  // form
submit validation\\n  if(form){\\n
form.addeventlistener(\\'submit\\',e=>{\\n      const
ok=p1&&p2&&p1.value===p2.value&&p1.value.length>=12;\\n      if(!ok){\\n
 e.preventdefault();\\n        updatematch();\\n
if(p1&&p1.value.length<12)p1.focus();\\n        else if(p2)p2.focus();\\n
  return;\\n      }\\n      if(submitbtn){\\n
submitbtn.disabled=true;\\n        submitbtn.innerhtml=\\'creating
account\u2026\\';\\n      }\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  // expiry countdown\\n
function renderexpirybadge(){\\n    if(!expirybadge)return;\\n    const
iso=expirybadge.title;\\n    if(!iso)return;\\n    const end=new date(iso);\\n
   if(isnan(end.gettime())){expirybadge.textcontent="expiry
unknown";return;}\\n    function tick(){\\n      const now=new date();\\n
let ms=end-now;\\n      const expired=ms<=0;\\n
if(expired)ms=math.abs(ms);\\n      const sec=math.floor(ms/1000),
m=math.floor(sec/60), h=math.floor(m/60), d=math.floor(h/24);\\n      let
text;\\n      if(expired){\\n        if(m<1)text="expired just now";\\n
else if(h<1)text=`expired ${m} min ago`;\\n        else if(d<1)text=`expired
${h} hr ago`;\\n        else text=`expired ${d} d ago`;\\n
expirybadge.style.background=\\'#fee2e2\\';\\n
expirybadge.style.color=\\'#b91c1c\\';\\n      }else{\\n
if(h<1)text=`expires in ${m} min`;\\n        else if(d<1)text=`expires in ${h}
hr`;\\n        else text=`expires in ${d} d`;\\n      }\\n
expirybadge.textcontent=text;\\n    }\\n    tick();\\n
setinterval(tick,30000);\\n  }\\n\\n  // event listeners\\n
if(nameinput)nameinput.addeventlistener(\\'input\\',updategreeting);\\n
if(p1){\\n    p1.addeventlistener(\\'input\\',updatestrength);\\n
p1.addeventlistener(\\'input\\',updatematch);\\n  }\\n
if(p2)p2.addeventlistener(\\'input\\',updatematch);\\n  \\n  // initial
updates\\n  updategreeting();\\n  updatestrength();\\n
renderexpirybadge();\\n})();\\n</script>\\n\\n      \\n      \\n      \\n
</main>\\n\\n    \\n    \\n  </div>\\n</div>\\n\\n<!-- inbox modal (fallback)
-->\\n<div class="modal fade" id="ccinbox" tabindex="-1"
aria-hidden="true">\\n  <div class="modal-dialog modal-dialog-scrollable
modal-xl">\\n    <div class="modal-content">\\n      <div
class="modal-header">\\n        <h5 class="modal-title cc-inbox-title"><i
class="bi bi-bell me-2"></i>notifications inbox</h5>\\n        <button
type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="modal"
aria-label="close"></button>\\n      </div>\\n      <div
class="modal-body">\\n        <div class="d-flex align-items-center
justify-content-between mb-2">\\n          <div class="text-muted">read-only
fallback (server inbox is down)</div>\\n          <div class="d-flex
gap-2">\\n            <button class="btn btn-sm btn-light"

-->\\n<script>window.cc_disable_legacy_mobile = 1;</script>\\n\\n<script

src="/static/js/mobile.js?v=1767716909" defer></script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /*
============================================================\\n     csrf:
resilient cookie+meta handling + global fetch shim\\n     - ensures we always
send a valid x-csrftoken header for post/put/patch/delete\\n     - fixes
"incorrect length" by avoiding empty headers\\n
============================================================ */\\n
(function(){\\n    function readcookie(name){\\n      const m =
document.cookie.match(new regexp(\\'(?:^|; )\\' +
name.replace(/[-[\\\\]{}()*+?.,\\\\\\\\^$|#\\\\\\\\s]/g,\\'\\\\\\\\$&\\') +
\\'=([^;]*)\\'));\\n      return m ? decodeuricomponent(m[1]) : \\'\\';\\n
}\\n    function setcookie(name, value, days){\\n      try{\\n        const d
= new date(); d.settime(d.gettime() + (days||1)*864e5);\\n
document.cookie = name + \\'=\\' + encodeuricomponent(value) + \\'; path=/;
samesite=lax; expires=\\' + d.toutcstring();\\n      }catch(_){}\\n    }\\n
function metatoken(){\\n      const el =
document.queryselector(\\'meta[name="csrf-token"]\\');\\n      const val = el
&& el.getattribute(\\'content\\');\\n      if (!val || val ===
\\'notprovided\\') return \\'\\';\\n      return val;\\n    }\\n    function
ensurecookiefrommeta(){\\n      // if neither cookie is present, but a meta
token exists, seed csrftoken\\n      const hascsrftoken =
!!readcookie(\\'csrftoken\\');\\n      const hascc =
!!readcookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\');\\n      if (!hascsrftoken && !hascc){\\n
   const t = metatoken();\\n        if (t && t.length > 20){\\n
setcookie(\\'csrftoken\\', t, 7);\\n        } else {\\n          // last
resort: no token available yet; don\\'t inject empty headers\\n        }\\n
  }\\n    }\\n    ensurecookiefrommeta();\\n\\n    function getcsrftoken(){\\n
     // prefer cookies; fall back to meta if needed\\n      return
readcookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\') || readcookie(\\'csrftoken\\') || metatoken()
|| \\'\\';\\n    }\\n\\n    // expose helper + urls + user flags\\n
window.cc = object.assign(window.cc || {}, {\\n      getcsrftoken,\\n
urls: object.assign({\\n        time_checkin_api:
"/inventory/api/time-checkin/"\\n      }, (window.cc && window.cc.urls) ||
{})\\n    });\\n\\n    // ---- global fetch shim: auto-attach csrf on
same-origin unsafe methods ----\\n    if (!window.__cc_fetch_shim__){\\n
window.__cc_fetch_shim__ = true;\\n      const _fetch =
window.fetch.bind(window);\\n\\n      function issameorigin(url){\\n
try{\\n          const u = new url(url, location.href);\\n          return
u.origin === location.origin;\\n        }catch(_){\\n          return true; //
request objects or relative urls: treat as same-origin\\n        }\\n
}\\n      function hasheader(h, name){\\n        if (!h) return false;\\n
  if (h instanceof headers) return h.has(name);\\n        const lower =
name.tolowercase();\\n        for (const k in h){ if
(object.prototype.hasownproperty.call(h,k) && k.tolowercase() === lower)
return true; }\\n        return false;\\n      }\\n      function setheader(h,
name, val){\\n        if (h instanceof headers) { h.set(name, val); return h;
}\\n        const clone = object.assign({}, h || {});\\n        clone[name] =
val;\\n        return clone;\\n      }\\n\\n      window.fetch =
function(input, init){\\n        init = init || {};\\n        let method =
(init.method || (input && input.method) || \\'get\\').touppercase();\\n
const same = (typeof input === \\'string\\') ? issameorigin(input) :
true;\\n\\n        if (same && method !== \\'get\\' && method !==
\\'head\\'){\\n          // only attach if we actually have a valid token;
never send empty string\\n          const token = getcsrftoken();\\n
if (token && token.length > 20){\\n            if (init.headers){\\n
   if (!hasheader(init.headers, \\'x-csrftoken\\')){\\n
init.headers = setheader(init.headers, \\'x-csrftoken\\', token);\\n
   }\\n            } else {\\n              init.headers = {\\'x-csrftoken\\':
token};\\n            }\\n          }\\n          // ensure cookies flow for
session auth\\n          if (!(\\'credentials\\' in init)) init.credentials =
\\'same-origin\\';\\n        }\\n        return _fetch(input, init);\\n
};\\n    }\\n  })();\\n</script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /* helpers (urls, roles)

</div>\\n</div>\\n\\n<script>\\n(function(){\\n  const shopName="Test
Shop";\\n  const defaultInvitee="Test Agent";\\n  const
form=document.getElementById(\\'inviteForm\\');\\n  const
nameInput=document.getElementById(\\'name\\');\\n  const
greeting=document.getElementById(\\'greeting\\');\\n  const
p1=document.getElementById(\\'id_password1\\');\\n  const
p2=document.getElementById(\\'id_password2\\');\\n  const
matchHint=document.getElementById(\\'matchHint\\');\\n  const
strengthHint=document.getElementById(\\'strengthHint\\');\\n  const
expiryBadge=document.getElementById(\\'expiryBadge\\');\\n  const
submitBtn=document.getElementById(\\'submitBtn\\');\\n\\n  // Update greeting
when name changes\\n  function updateGreeting(){\\n
if(!greeting||!nameInput) return;\\n    const
name=nameInput.value.trim()||defaultInvitee||\\'there\\';\\n
greeting.textContent=`Hi ${name}, welcome to ${shopName}.`;\\n  }\\n\\n  //
Password strength\\n  function strength(s){\\n    let n=0;\\n
if(s.length>=12)n++;\\n    if(/[a-z]/.test(s)&&/[A-Z]/.test(s))n++;\\n
if(/\\\\d/.test(s))n++;\\n    if(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(s))n++;\\n    return
n;\\n  }\\n  function updateStrength(){\\n    if(!p1||!strengthHint)return;\\n
   const sc=strength(p1.value||"");\\n    let msg="";\\n    if(!p1.value)
msg="";\\n    else if(sc<=1) msg="\u26a0\ufe0f Too weak - needs 12+ chars,
upper, lower, digit, symbol";\\n    else if(sc===2) msg="\u26a0\ufe0f Weak -
missing some requirements";\\n    else if(sc===3) msg="\u2713 Good
password";\\n    else msg="\u2713 Strong password";\\n
strengthHint.textContent=msg;\\n    strengthHint.className=(sc<=2?"text-danger"
:sc===3?"text-warning":"text-success");\\n  }\\n\\n  // Password match\\n
function updateMatch(){\\n    if(!p1||!p2||!matchHint)return;\\n
if(!p1.value||!p2.value){matchHint.style.display=\\'none\\';return;}\\n
const ok=p1.value===p2.value && p1.value.length>=12;\\n
matchHint.style.display=\\'block\\';\\n    matchHint.className="form-text
"+(ok?"text-success":"text-danger");\\n    matchHint.textContent=ok?"\u2713
Passwords match":"\u26a0\ufe0f Passwords don\\'t match";\\n  }\\n\\n  //
Show/hide password toggles\\n  document.querySelectorAll(\\'button[data-toggle=
"reveal"]\\').forEach(btn=>{\\n    btn.addEventListener(\\'click\\',()=>{\\n
   const sel=btn.getAttribute(\\'data-target\\');\\n      const
input=document.querySelector(sel);\\n      if(!input)return;\\n      const
isPwd=input.getAttribute(\\'type\\')===\\'password\\';\\n
input.setAttribute(\\'type\\',isPwd?\\'text\\':\\'password\\');\\n
btn.textContent=isPwd?\\'Hide\\':\\'Show\\';\\n    });\\n  });\\n\\n  // Form
submit validation\\n  if(form){\\n
form.addEventListener(\\'submit\\',e=>{\\n      const
ok=p1&&p2&&p1.value===p2.value&&p1.value.length>=12;\\n      if(!ok){\\n
 e.preventDefault();\\n        updateMatch();\\n

  return;\\n      }\\n      if(submitBtn){\\n

account\u2026\\';\\n      }\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  // Expiry countdown\\n
function renderExpiryBadge(){\\n    if(!expiryBadge)return;\\n    const
iso=expiryBadge.title;\\n    if(!iso)return;\\n    const end=new Date(iso);\\n
   if(isNaN(end.getTime())){expiryBadge.textContent="expiry
unknown";return;}\\n    function tick(){\\n      const now=new Date();\\n
let ms=end-now;\\n      const expired=ms<=0;\\n
if(expired)ms=Math.abs(ms);\\n      const sec=Math.floor(ms/1000),
m=Math.floor(sec/60), h=Math.floor(m/60), d=Math.floor(h/24);\\n      let
text;\\n      if(expired){\\n        if(m<1)text="expired just now";\\n
else if(h<1)text=`expired ${m} min ago`;\\n        else if(d<1)text=`expired
${h} hr ago`;\\n        else text=`expired ${d} d ago`;\\n
expiryBadge.style.background=\\'#fee2e2\\';\\n
expiryBadge.style.color=\\'#b91c1c\\';\\n      }else{\\n
if(h<1)text=`expires in ${m} min`;\\n        else if(d<1)text=`expires in ${h}
hr`;\\n        else text=`expires in ${d} d`;\\n      }\\n
expiryBadge.textContent=text;\\n    }\\n    tick();\\n
setInterval(tick,30000);\\n  }\\n\\n  // Event listeners\\n

p1.addEventListener(\\'input\\',updateMatch);\\n  }\\n
if(p2)p2.addEventListener(\\'input\\',updateMatch);\\n  \\n  // Initial
updates\\n  updateGreeting();\\n  updateStrength();\\n
renderExpiryBadge();\\n})();\\n</script>\\n\\n      \\n      \\n      \\n

-->\\n<script>window.CC_DISABLE_LEGACY_MOBILE = 1;</script>\\n\\n<script
src="/static/js/app.js?v=1767716909" defer></script>\\n<script
src="/static/js/mobile.js?v=1767716909" defer></script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /*
============================================================\\n     CSRF:
resilient cookie+meta handling + global fetch shim\\n     - Ensures we ALWAYS
send a valid X-CSRFToken header for POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE\\n     - Fixes
"incorrect length" by avoiding empty headers\\n
============================================================ */\\n
(function(){\\n    function readCookie(name){\\n      const m =
document.cookie.match(new RegExp(\\'(?:^|; )\\' +
name.replace(/[-[\\\\]{}()*+?.,\\\\\\\\^$|#\\\\\\\\s]/g,\\'\\\\\\\\$&\\') +
\\'=([^;]*)\\'));\\n      return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : \\'\\';\\n
}\\n    function setCookie(name, value, days){\\n      try{\\n        const d
= new Date(); d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days||1)*864e5);\\n
document.cookie = name + \\'=\\' + encodeURIComponent(value) + \\'; path=/;
SameSite=Lax; expires=\\' + d.toUTCString();\\n      }catch(_){}\\n    }\\n
function metaToken(){\\n      const el =
document.querySelector(\\'meta[name="csrf-token"]\\');\\n      const val = el
&& el.getAttribute(\\'content\\');\\n      if (!val || val ===
\\'NOTPROVIDED\\') return \\'\\';\\n      return val;\\n    }\\n    function
ensureCookieFromMeta(){\\n      // If neither cookie is present, but a meta
token exists, seed csrftoken\\n      const hasCsrftoken =
!!readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\');\\n      const hasCC =
!!readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\');\\n      if (!hasCsrftoken && !hasCC){\\n
   const t = metaToken();\\n        if (t && t.length > 20){\\n
setCookie(\\'csrftoken\\', t, 7);\\n        } else {\\n          // last
resort: no token available yet; don\\'t inject empty headers\\n        }\\n
  }\\n    }\\n    ensureCookieFromMeta();\\n\\n    function getCsrfToken(){\\n
     // Prefer cookies; fall back to meta if needed\\n      return
readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\') || readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\') || metaToken()
|| \\'\\';\\n    }\\n\\n    // Expose helper + URLs + user flags\\n
window.CC = Object.assign(window.CC || {}, {\\n      getCsrfToken,\\n
URLS: Object.assign({\\n        time_checkin_api:
"/inventory/api/time-checkin/"\\n      }, (window.CC && window.CC.URLS) ||
{})\\n    });\\n\\n    // ---- Global fetch shim: auto-attach CSRF on
same-origin unsafe methods ----\\n    if (!window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__){\\n
window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__ = true;\\n      const _fetch =
window.fetch.bind(window);\\n\\n      function isSameOrigin(url){\\n
try{\\n          const u = new URL(url, location.href);\\n          return
u.origin === location.origin;\\n        }catch(_){\\n          return true; //
Request objects or relative URLs: treat as same-origin\\n        }\\n
}\\n      function hasHeader(h, name){\\n        if (!h) return false;\\n
  if (h instanceof Headers) return h.has(name);\\n        const lower =
name.toLowerCase();\\n        for (const k in h){ if
(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(h,k) && k.toLowerCase() === lower)
return true; }\\n        return false;\\n      }\\n      function setHeader(h,
name, val){\\n        if (h instanceof Headers) { h.set(name, val); return h;
}\\n        const clone = Object.assign({}, h || {});\\n        clone[name] =
val;\\n        return clone;\\n      }\\n\\n      window.fetch =
function(input, init){\\n        init = init || {};\\n        let method =
(init.method || (input && input.method) || \\'GET\\').toUpperCase();\\n
const same = (typeof input === \\'string\\') ? isSameOrigin(input) :
true;\\n\\n        if (same && method !== \\'GET\\' && method !==
\\'HEAD\\'){\\n          // Only attach if we actually have a valid token;
never send empty string\\n          const token = getCsrfToken();\\n
if (token && token.length > 20){\\n            if (init.headers){\\n
   if (!hasHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\')){\\n
init.headers = setHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\', token);\\n
   }\\n            } else {\\n              init.headers = {\\'X-CSRFToken\\':
token};\\n            }\\n          }\\n          // Ensure cookies flow for
session auth\\n          if (!(\\'credentials\\' in init)) init.credentials =
\\'same-origin\\';\\n        }\\n        return _fetch(input, init);\\n
};\\n    }\\n  })();\\n</script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /* Helpers (URLs, roles)

Password strength\\n  function strength(s){\\n    let n=0;\\n
if(s.length>=12)n++;\\n    if(/[a-z]/.test(s)&&/[A-Z]/.test(s))n++;\\n
if(/\\\\d/.test(s))n++;\\n    if(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(s))n++;\\n    return
n;\\n  }\\n  function updateStrength(){\\n    if(!p1||!strengthHint)return;\\n
   const sc=strength(p1.value||"");\\n    let msg="";\\n    if(!p1.value)
msg="";\\n    else if(sc<=1) msg="\u26a0\ufe0f Too weak - needs 12+ chars,
upper, lower, digit, symbol";\\n    else if(sc===2) msg="\u26a0\ufe0f Weak -
missing some requirements";\\n    else if(sc===3) msg="\u2713 Good
password";\\n    else msg="\u2713 Strong password";\\n
strengthHint.textContent=msg;\\n    strengthHint.className=(sc<=2?"text-danger"
:sc===3?"text-warning":"text-success");\\n  }\\n\\n  // Password match\\n
function updateMatch(){\\n    if(!p1||!p2||!matchHint)return;\\n
if(!p1.value||!p2.value){matchHint.style.display=\\'none\\';return;}\\n
const ok=p1.value===p2.value && p1.value.length>=12;\\n
matchHint.style.display=\\'block\\';\\n    matchHint.className="form-text
"+(ok?"text-success":"text-danger");\\n    matchHint.textContent=ok?"\u2713
Passwords match":"\u26a0\ufe0f Passwords don\\'t match";\\n  }\\n\\n  //
Show/hide password toggles\\n  document.querySelectorAll(\\'button[data-toggle=
"reveal"]\\').forEach(btn=>{\\n    btn.addEventListener(\\'click\\',()=>{\\n
   const sel=btn.getAttribute(\\'data-target\\');\\n      const

  return;\\n      }\\n      if(submitBtn){\\n

function renderExpiryBadge(){\\n    if(!expiryBadge)return;\\n    const
iso=expiryBadge.title;\\n    if(!iso)return;\\n    const end=new Date(iso);\\n
   if(isNaN(end.getTime())){expiryBadge.textContent="expiry
unknown";return;}\\n    function tick(){\\n      const now=new Date();\\n
let ms=end-now;\\n      const expired=ms<=0;\\n
if(expired)ms=Math.abs(ms);\\n      const sec=Math.floor(ms/1000),
m=Math.floor(sec/60), h=Math.floor(m/60), d=Math.floor(h/24);\\n      let
text;\\n      if(expired){\\n        if(m<1)text="expired just now";\\n
else if(h<1)text=`expired ${m} min ago`;\\n        else if(d<1)text=`expired
${h} hr ago`;\\n        else text=`expired ${d} d ago`;\\n
expiryBadge.style.background=\\'#fee2e2\\';\\n
expiryBadge.style.color=\\'#b91c1c\\';\\n      }else{\\n
if(h<1)text=`expires in ${m} min`;\\n        else if(d<1)text=`expires in ${h}
hr`;\\n        else text=`expires in ${d} d`;\\n      }\\n
expiryBadge.textContent=text;\\n    }\\n    tick();\\n
setInterval(tick,30000);\\n  }\\n\\n  // Event listeners\\n

if(p2)p2.addEventListener(\\'input\\',updateMatch);\\n  \\n  // Initial
updates\\n  updateGreeting();\\n  updateStrength();\\n

-->\\n<script>window.CC_DISABLE_LEGACY_MOBILE = 1;</script>\\n\\n<script
src="/static/js/app.js?v=1767716909" defer></script>\\n<script
src="/static/js/mobile.js?v=1767716909" defer></script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /*
============================================================\\n     CSRF:
resilient cookie+meta handling + global fetch shim\\n     - Ensures we ALWAYS
send a valid X-CSRFToken header for POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE\\n     - Fixes
"incorrect length" by avoiding empty headers\\n
============================================================ */\\n
(function(){\\n    function readCookie(name){\\n      const m =
document.cookie.match(new RegExp(\\'(?:^|; )\\' +
name.replace(/[-[\\\\]{}()*+?.,\\\\\\\\^$|#\\\\\\\\s]/g,\\'\\\\\\\\$&\\') +
\\'=([^;]*)\\'));\\n      return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : \\'\\';\\n
}\\n    function setCookie(name, value, days){\\n      try{\\n        const d
= new Date(); d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days||1)*864e5);\\n
document.cookie = name + \\'=\\' + encodeURIComponent(value) + \\'; path=/;
SameSite=Lax; expires=\\' + d.toUTCString();\\n      }catch(_){}\\n    }\\n
function metaToken(){\\n      const el =
document.querySelector(\\'meta[name="csrf-token"]\\');\\n      const val = el
&& el.getAttribute(\\'content\\');\\n      if (!val || val ===
\\'NOTPROVIDED\\') return \\'\\';\\n      return val;\\n    }\\n    function
ensureCookieFromMeta(){\\n      // If neither cookie is present, but a meta
token exists, seed csrftoken\\n      const hasCsrftoken =
!!readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\');\\n      const hasCC =
!!readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\');\\n      if (!hasCsrftoken && !hasCC){\\n
   const t = metaToken();\\n        if (t && t.length > 20){\\n
setCookie(\\'csrftoken\\', t, 7);\\n        } else {\\n          // last
resort: no token available yet; don\\'t inject empty headers\\n        }\\n
  }\\n    }\\n    ensureCookieFromMeta();\\n\\n    function getCsrfToken(){\\n
     // Prefer cookies; fall back to meta if needed\\n      return
readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\') || readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\') || metaToken()
|| \\'\\';\\n    }\\n\\n    // Expose helper + URLs + user flags\\n
window.CC = Object.assign(window.CC || {}, {\\n      getCsrfToken,\\n
URLS: Object.assign({\\n        time_checkin_api:
"/inventory/api/time-checkin/"\\n      }, (window.CC && window.CC.URLS) ||
{})\\n    });\\n\\n    // ---- Global fetch shim: auto-attach CSRF on
same-origin unsafe methods ----\\n    if (!window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__){\\n
window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__ = true;\\n      const _fetch =
window.fetch.bind(window);\\n\\n      function isSameOrigin(url){\\n
try{\\n          const u = new URL(url, location.href);\\n          return
u.origin === location.origin;\\n        }catch(_){\\n          return true; //
Request objects or relative URLs: treat as same-origin\\n        }\\n
}\\n      function hasHeader(h, name){\\n        if (!h) return false;\\n
  if (h instanceof Headers) return h.has(name);\\n        const lower =
name.toLowerCase();\\n        for (const k in h){ if
(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(h,k) && k.toLowerCase() === lower)
return true; }\\n        return false;\\n      }\\n      function setHeader(h,
name, val){\\n        if (h instanceof Headers) { h.set(name, val); return h;
}\\n        const clone = Object.assign({}, h || {});\\n        clone[name] =
val;\\n        return clone;\\n      }\\n\\n      window.fetch =
function(input, init){\\n        init = init || {};\\n        let method =
(init.method || (input && input.method) || \\'GET\\').toUpperCase();\\n
const same = (typeof input === \\'string\\') ? isSameOrigin(input) :
true;\\n\\n        if (same && method !== \\'GET\\' && method !==
\\'HEAD\\'){\\n          // Only attach if we actually have a valid token;
never send empty string\\n          const token = getCsrfToken();\\n
if (token && token.length > 20){\\n            if (init.headers){\\n
   if (!hasHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\')){\\n
init.headers = setHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\', token);\\n
   }\\n            } else {\\n              init.headers = {\\'X-CSRFToken\\':
token};\\n            }\\n          }\\n          // Ensure cookies flow for
session auth\\n          if (!(\\'credentials\\' in init)) init.credentials =
\\'same-origin\\';\\n        }\\n        return _fetch(input, init);\\n
};\\n    }\\n  })();\\n</script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /* Helpers (URLs, roles)
*/\\n  (function(){\\n    window.CC = Object.assign(window.CC || {}, {\\n

  }\\n    function toggle(){\\n      if (busy) return;\\n      const now =

   openState = !openState;\\n      applyState();\\n

legacy sidebar code BEFORE loading your scripts
-->\\n<script>window.CC_DISABLE_LEGACY_MOBILE = 1;</script>\\n\\n<script
src="/static/js/app.js?v=1767717127" defer></script>\\n<script
src="/static/js/mobile.js?v=1767717127" defer></script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /*
============================================================\\n     CSRF:
resilient cookie+meta handling + global fetch shim\\n     - Ensures we ALWAYS
send a valid X-CSRFToken header for POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE\\n     - Fixes
"incorrect length" by avoiding empty headers\\n
============================================================ */\\n
(function(){\\n    function readCookie(name){\\n      const m =
document.cookie.match(new RegExp(\\'(?:^|; )\\' +
name.replace(/[-[\\\\]{}()*+?.,\\\\\\\\^$|#\\\\\\\\s]/g,\\'\\\\\\\\$&\\') +
\\'=([^;]*)\\'));\\n      return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : \\'\\';\\n
}\\n    function setCookie(name, value, days){\\n      try{\\n        const d
= new Date(); d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days||1)*864e5);\\n
document.cookie = name + \\'=\\' + encodeURIComponent(value) + \\'; path=/;
SameSite=Lax; expires=\\' + d.toUTCString();\\n      }catch(_){}\\n    }\\n
function metaToken(){\\n      const el =
document.querySelector(\\'meta[name="csrf-token"]\\');\\n      const val = el
&& el.getAttribute(\\'content\\');\\n      if (!val || val ===
\\'NOTPROVIDED\\') return \\'\\';\\n      return val;\\n    }\\n    function
ensureCookieFromMeta(){\\n      // If neither cookie is present, but a meta
token exists, seed csrftoken\\n      const hasCsrftoken =
!!readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\');\\n      const hasCC =
!!readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\');\\n      if (!hasCsrftoken && !hasCC){\\n
   const t = metaToken();\\n        if (t && t.length > 20){\\n
setCookie(\\'csrftoken\\', t, 7);\\n        } else {\\n          // last
resort: no token available yet; don\\'t inject empty headers\\n        }\\n
  }\\n    }\\n    ensureCookieFromMeta();\\n\\n    function getCsrfToken(){\\n
     // Prefer cookies; fall back to meta if needed\\n      return
readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\') || readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\') || metaToken()
|| \\'\\';\\n    }\\n\\n    // Expose helper + URLs + user flags\\n
window.CC = Object.assign(window.CC || {}, {\\n      getCsrfToken,\\n
URLS: Object.assign({\\n        time_checkin_api:
"/inventory/api/time-checkin/"\\n      }, (window.CC && window.CC.URLS) ||
{})\\n    });\\n\\n    // ---- Global fetch shim: auto-attach CSRF on
same-origin unsafe methods ----\\n    if (!window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__){\\n
window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__ = true;\\n      const _fetch =
window.fetch.bind(window);\\n\\n      function isSameOrigin(url){\\n
try{\\n          const u = new URL(url, location.href);\\n          return
u.origin === location.origin;\\n        }catch(_){\\n          return true; //
Request objects or relative URLs: treat as same-origin\\n        }\\n
}\\n      function hasHeader(h, name){\\n        if (!h) return false;\\n
  if (h instanceof Headers) return h.has(name);\\n        const lower =
name.toLowerCase();\\n        for (const k in h){ if
(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(h,k) && k.toLowerCase() === lower)
return true; }\\n        return false;\\n      }\\n      function setHeader(h,
name, val){\\n        if (h instanceof Headers) { h.set(name, val); return h;
}\\n        const clone = Object.assign({}, h || {});\\n        clone[name] =
val;\\n        return clone;\\n      }\\n\\n      window.fetch =
function(input, init){\\n        init = init || {};\\n        let method =
(init.method || (input && input.method) || \\'GET\\').toUpperCase();\\n
const same = (typeof input === \\'string\\') ? isSameOrigin(input) :
true;\\n\\n        if (same && method !== \\'GET\\' && method !==
\\'HEAD\\'){\\n          // Only attach if we actually have a valid token;
never send empty string\\n          const token = getCsrfToken();\\n
if (token && token.length > 20){\\n            if (init.headers){\\n
   if (!hasHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\')){\\n
init.headers = setHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\', token);\\n
   }\\n            } else {\\n              init.headers = {\\'X-CSRFToken\\':
token};\\n            }\\n          }\\n          // Ensure cookies flow for
session auth\\n          if (!(\\'credentials\\' in init)) init.credentials =
\\'same-origin\\';\\n        }\\n        return _fetch(input, init);\\n
};\\n    }\\n  })();\\n</script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /* Helpers (URLs, roles)
*/\\n  (function(){\\n    window.CC = Object.assign(window.CC || {}, {\\n

  }\\n    function toggle(){\\n      if (busy) return;\\n      const now =

   openState = !openState;\\n      applyState();\\n

// SINGLE handler: pointerup only (no click/touchend duplicates)\\n

});\\n\\n    backdrop.addEventListener(\\'click\\', (e)=>{ e.preventDefault();

-->\\n<script>window.CC_DISABLE_LEGACY_MOBILE = 1;</script>\\n\\n<script
src="/static/js/app.js?v=1767717128" defer></script>\\n<script
src="/static/js/mobile.js?v=1767717128" defer></script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /*
============================================================\\n     CSRF:
resilient cookie+meta handling + global fetch shim\\n     - Ensures we ALWAYS
send a valid X-CSRFToken header for POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE\\n     - Fixes
"incorrect length" by avoiding empty headers\\n
============================================================ */\\n
(function(){\\n    function readCookie(name){\\n      const m =
document.cookie.match(new RegExp(\\'(?:^|; )\\' +
name.replace(/[-[\\\\]{}()*+?.,\\\\\\\\^$|#\\\\\\\\s]/g,\\'\\\\\\\\$&\\') +
\\'=([^;]*)\\'));\\n      return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : \\'\\';\\n
}\\n    function setCookie(name, value, days){\\n      try{\\n        const d
= new Date(); d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days||1)*864e5);\\n
document.cookie = name + \\'=\\' + encodeURIComponent(value) + \\'; path=/;
SameSite=Lax; expires=\\' + d.toUTCString();\\n      }catch(_){}\\n    }\\n
function metaToken(){\\n      const el =
document.querySelector(\\'meta[name="csrf-token"]\\');\\n      const val = el
&& el.getAttribute(\\'content\\');\\n      if (!val || val ===
\\'NOTPROVIDED\\') return \\'\\';\\n      return val;\\n    }\\n    function
ensureCookieFromMeta(){\\n      // If neither cookie is present, but a meta
token exists, seed csrftoken\\n      const hasCsrftoken =
!!readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\');\\n      const hasCC =
!!readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\');\\n      if (!hasCsrftoken && !hasCC){\\n
   const t = metaToken();\\n        if (t && t.length > 20){\\n
setCookie(\\'csrftoken\\', t, 7);\\n        } else {\\n          // last
resort: no token available yet; don\\'t inject empty headers\\n        }\\n
  }\\n    }\\n    ensureCookieFromMeta();\\n\\n    function getCsrfToken(){\\n
     // Prefer cookies; fall back to meta if needed\\n      return
readCookie(\\'cc_csrftoken\\') || readCookie(\\'csrftoken\\') || metaToken()
|| \\'\\';\\n    }\\n\\n    // Expose helper + URLs + user flags\\n
window.CC = Object.assign(window.CC || {}, {\\n      getCsrfToken,\\n
URLS: Object.assign({\\n        time_checkin_api:
"/inventory/api/time-checkin/"\\n      }, (window.CC && window.CC.URLS) ||
{})\\n    });\\n\\n    // ---- Global fetch shim: auto-attach CSRF on
same-origin unsafe methods ----\\n    if (!window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__){\\n
window.__CC_FETCH_SHIM__ = true;\\n      const _fetch =
window.fetch.bind(window);\\n\\n      function isSameOrigin(url){\\n
try{\\n          const u = new URL(url, location.href);\\n          return
u.origin === location.origin;\\n        }catch(_){\\n          return true; //
Request objects or relative URLs: treat as same-origin\\n        }\\n
}\\n      function hasHeader(h, name){\\n        if (!h) return false;\\n
  if (h instanceof Headers) return h.has(name);\\n        const lower =
name.toLowerCase();\\n        for (const k in h){ if
(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(h,k) && k.toLowerCase() === lower)
return true; }\\n        return false;\\n      }\\n      function setHeader(h,
name, val){\\n        if (h instanceof Headers) { h.set(name, val); return h;
}\\n        const clone = Object.assign({}, h || {});\\n        clone[name] =
val;\\n        return clone;\\n      }\\n\\n      window.fetch =
function(input, init){\\n        init = init || {};\\n        let method =
(init.method || (input && input.method) || \\'GET\\').toUpperCase();\\n
const same = (typeof input === \\'string\\') ? isSameOrigin(input) :
true;\\n\\n        if (same && method !== \\'GET\\' && method !==
\\'HEAD\\'){\\n          // Only attach if we actually have a valid token;
never send empty string\\n          const token = getCsrfToken();\\n
if (token && token.length > 20){\\n            if (init.headers){\\n
   if (!hasHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\')){\\n
init.headers = setHeader(init.headers, \\'X-CSRFToken\\', token);\\n
   }\\n            } else {\\n              init.headers = {\\'X-CSRFToken\\':
token};\\n            }\\n          }\\n          // Ensure cookies flow for
session auth\\n          if (!(\\'credentials\\' in init)) init.credentials =
\\'same-origin\\';\\n        }\\n        return _fetch(input, init);\\n
};\\n    }\\n  })();\\n</script>\\n\\n<script>\\n  /* Helpers (URLs, roles)

  }\\n    function toggle(){\\n      if (busy) return;\\n      const now =

   openState = !openState;\\n      applyState();\\n
